### Unsealed Wrath (1310735)
- Spell row 1310735: Description_lang: Reduces the Mana cost of your Seal spells by $s1%, and when you replace your Seal of Command, Seal of Righteousness, Seal of Fury, or Seal of Justice with a different Seal, you gain an Echo of that Seal. Your next melee attack applies the replaced Seal's effects, consuming the Echo. -> Your melee attacks store a copy of your active Seal for $1311701d, stacking up to $1311701u times. Landing an attack with a different Seal consumes all stacks, giving the stored Seal its normal chance to activate once per stack.
